- **causal**: Builds a **causal graph**: which factors the investigation says led to which. ProbeMatrix writes a causal explanation for each finding ("Enabled by SV 1.4 and OR 2.2"); causal reads every explanation, picks out the factor codes it names, and counts each link from one factor to another. A link always points from the higher HFACS level to the lower one (from the organisation towards the act), whiche ([ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach](https://github.com/ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach/blob/HEAD/docs/manual/commands/causal.md))
- **causal-check**: Run a structured causal inference review on a task or artifact. ([oishizawads/ai-os-univ](https://github.com/oishizawads/ai-os-univ/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/causal-check.md))
- **causal-design**: Design or audit the identification strategy for an observational study. Use when the task concerns estimands, causal assumptions, threats to identification, or defensible research design rather than model implementation. ([flonat/flonat-research](https://github.com/flonat/flonat-research/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/causal-design.md) · ★153)
- **causal-discovery-expert**: Activate the `causal-discovery-expert` agent. You are now operating as an expert in Causal Discovery, ALVGL decomposition, and DAG constraints. Apply deep knowledge of structural learning, ADMM optimization, and acyclicity enforcement to the current task in the 'consider' project. ([xupit3r/consider](https://github.com/xupit3r/consider/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/causal-discovery-expert.md))
- **causal-inference-science**: Quick reference for `causal inference science`. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/causal-inference-science.md) · ★1)
- **causal-item**: Prints the strongest links of the [causal](causal.md) graph as one ready-made **graph item** for a learning pack: a JSON object with `"kind": "graph"`, its nodes (each factor with its level and name), its edges (with the number of mentions) and the Finding IDs it cites. The agent pastes it into a pack section, so the causal picture in the pack is drawn from the data, not retyped. ([ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach](https://github.com/ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach/blob/HEAD/docs/manual/commands/causal-item.md))

- **ceo-loop**: Activate the CEO Decision Loop in ORBIT mode. Starts a continuous multi-iteration governance cycle: OBSERVE pending decisions → REASON with precedent → BUILD decisions → INSPECT retrograde → TURN for next iteration. The Stop hook keeps the loop alive until the queue is empty or limits are reached. Usage: /nxtg-forge:ceo-loop [max-iterations] [time-limit-minutes] ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop.md) · ★7)
- **ceo-loop-cancel**: Cancel the active CEO Decision Loop gracefully. Sets the loop to inactive, writes a final session summary to the progress file, and logs session statistics. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop-cancel.md) · ★7)

Sem confirmação humana (D-45). Ex.: /cerebro-descobrir ([bittencourtthulio/expxyoutube](https://github.com/bittencourtthulio/expxyoutube/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cerebro-descobrir.md))
- **cerebro-doctor**: Validate the Cerebro workflow configuration. ([yelaco/claude-xmen](https://github.com/yelaco/claude-xmen/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cerebro-doctor.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cerebro-index**: Create or refresh `.cerebro/project-context.md` for this repository. ([yelaco/claude-xmen](https://github.com/yelaco/claude-xmen/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cerebro-index.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cerebro-plan**: Plan this work: $ARGUMENTS ([yelaco/claude-xmen](https://github.com/yelaco/claude-xmen/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cerebro-plan.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cerebro-reset**: Wipe the Cerebro runtime and start fresh. This command destroys all execution history, plans, notepads, and active state. Use it when a run is irreparably stuck or you want a clean slate before a new mission. ([yelaco/claude-xmen](https://github.com/yelaco/claude-xmen/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cerebro-reset.md) · ★2)
- **cerebro-setup**: Run after cloning the template or any time the installation feels misconfigured. Wires `CLAUDE.md` correctly and checks whether the upstream template has a newer release. ([yelaco/claude-xmen](https://github.com/yelaco/claude-xmen/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cerebro-setup.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
